# Roadmap

Work top-to-bottom; `/dev-cycle` picks the first unchecked item. Check items off as they land (with tests). Keep milestones honest — an item isn't done until it works in the running app **and** over the control protocol.

## M0 — Foundation ✅
- [x] Repo scaffold, docs, agent fleet, skills
- [x] SPM monorepo: DAWCore / DAWEngine / DAWControl / AIServices / DAWApp
- [x] Domain model v0 (project, tracks, clips, transport, mixer state)
- [x] Control WebSocket server skeleton + command router
- [x] MCP server skeleton with core tool surface
- [x] App shell: dark theme, transport bar, glowing meter component

## M1 — Core audio engine ✅
- [x] Audio file import (wav/aiff/mp3/m4a) onto tracks
- [x] Sample-accurate multitrack playback via engine graph
- [x] Transport: play/stop/seek/loop region, position broadcast to UI + control clients
- [x] Per-track and master metering (peak/RMS) driving the glow meters
- [x] Master volume, per-track volume/pan/mute/solo wired to engine
- [x] Offline render (mixdown to wav) — `render.mixdown` command
- [x] Engine test harness: offline-render assertions (null test, gain test) — determinism null (exact 0.0) + unity-vs-source + gain suite; golden-master null noted as future addition

## M2 — Recording & project persistence
- [x] Record from default input onto armed tracks (`track.setArm`, `transport.record`, host-time-aligned takes) — no round-trip latency compensation yet (M4 PDC)
- [x] Input device enumeration/selection (`input.listDevices`, `input.setDevice`) — pinned-device capture fixed 2026-07-05 (benign config-change triage + first-buffer watchdog; live E2E green for default-uid pin, ZoomAudioDevice pin, and unpinned)
- [x] Punch in/out windows (`transport.setPunch`, writer accept-window)
- [x] Metronome/click with count-in
- [x] `.dawproj` bundle format: versioned JSON + media dir; open/save/autosave
- [x] Undo/redo across all commands (coalescing snapshot journal in DAWCore; `edit.undo`/`edit.redo`, Edit-menu ⌘Z/⇧⌘Z)

## M3 — MIDI & instruments
- [x] (i) MIDI domain + commands — `Clip.notes` payload (`MIDINote`: pitch/velocity/startBeat/lengthBeats, clamped, canonically ordered, mutually exclusive with audio media); `clip.addMIDI` / `clip.setNotes` / `clip.remove` on the control protocol (whole-array note set, per-clip undo coalescing); additive v1 persistence; instrument-track-only rule
- [x] (ii) Piano-roll editor (Simple + Pro views) — note draw/move/resize/velocity, snap grid, driving `clip.setNotes`; timeline clip lanes + `debug.captureUI` self-render verification
- [x] (iii) Sample-accurate MIDI scheduler — settles the sequencer clock decision (one `AVAudioSourceNode` per instrument track; host-delta live epoch / latched offline epoch), lock-free event delivery via an immutable schedule behind a C11 atomic pointer (`CAtomics` shim, ≥1 s retire bin), all-notes-off flush flag on every stop/seek/tempo/loop-wrap/reschedule, `InstrumentRendering` seam (AU-shaped for M3 vi) with EventCaptureInstrument + TestToneInstrument; validated by frame-exact offline event-timestamp tests
- [x] (iv) Built-in poly synth — 16-voice subtractive synth (PolyBLEP saw/square, triangle, sine; linear ADSR; Simper SVF low-pass) behind `InstrumentRendering`; `Track.instrument: InstrumentDescriptor?` (additive, nil ⇒ polySynth default), `track.setInstrument` control command + `track_set_instrument` MCP tool with partial-update semantics; RT-safe in-place param changes (atomic POD publish, held notes survive), kind switch = node rebuild + reschedule
- [x] (v) Built-in sampler — key zones (`SamplerZone`: audio file, root pitch, span, gain) under the same `InstrumentDescriptor`; pitched playback via linear-interp resampling (2^(Δsemitones/12) × rate ratio), one-shot mode, anti-click attack/release; zones structural (rebuild) vs scalars in-place (atomic); zone media joins the `.dawproj` bundle copy (relative `media/` refs, dedupe, `mediaFilesCopied`); `sampler` object on `track.setInstrument` + MCP
- [x] (vi-a) AU instrument hosting (headless) — `HostedAUInstrument` adapter behind the `InstrumentRendering` seam (our scheduler stays the only clock; events via `scheduleMIDIEventBlock` at in-quantum offsets; flush → CC123+120), `AUHostRegistry` (timeout-raced async prepare, strict-select/graceful-load: missing AU → silent placeholder + `status`), descriptor kind `audioUnit` with component identity + fullState base64 in `.dawproj`, `instrument.listAudioUnits` + `track.setInstrument audioUnit:` + MCP; `renderMixdown` now async (offline renders instantiate fresh AU instances); out-of-process AUv3 reliability deferred to M9 app bundling
- [x] (vi-b) AU plugin UI windows — `requestViewController`/v2 view hosting in floating glass-chrome windows; open/close over the control protocol — split 2026-07-10 per `docs/research/design-vi-b-au-windows.md` (DESIGN SETTLED — the implementation contract; unblocked by pkg-a's real app bundle):
  - [x] (vi-b-1) Plumbing + generic body — engine accessors for the live `AUAudioUnit` (concrete-class-only boundary exception) + registry `onRelease` invalidation seam; `plugin.openUI/closeUI/listOpenUIs` through a typed async `PluginUIControlling` seam (headless-honest errors); `PluginWindowLedger` (DAWAppKit, headless) + `PluginWindowManager`/`NSPanel` glass chrome (cyan/neutral, never violet); `AUGenericViewController` body only; `debug.captureUI {target:"plugin"}`; mixer/instrument open-window buttons; 3 MCP tools; headless suites + orchestrator live gate on system AUs (DLSMusicDevice/AUDelay), fully provable on the bare binary
  - [x] (vi-b-2) Vendor views — full resolution ladder (`requestViewController` w/ 5 s once-gate timeout race → v2 `kAudioUnitProperty_CocoaUI` via `AUAudioUnitV2Bridge` + `AUViewProbe` in DAWEngine → `AUGenericViewController` fallback, never-failing), `BodyHostViewController` wrapping the vendor `AUCocoaUIBase` view, v3 `preferredContentSize` sizing + `frameDidChangeNotification` resize-follow (reentrancy-guarded, clamped in-bounds); §10 de-risk spike resolved by inventory (NO AUv3 installed — the out-of-process AUv3 custom-view leg is SKIPPED-recorded, never faked); live gate on system units proves both branches — DLSMusicDevice/AUDelay → `body:"custom"` (real vendor views inside our chrome), AUMatrixReverb → `body:"generic"` — bare binary AND `dist/DAWPro.app` bundle identity; `docs/PACKAGING.md` note
- [x] (vii) MIDI hardware input — CoreMIDI UMP protocol-1.0 port, omni-connect + hot-plug; live thru on armed instrument tracks via per-renderer SPSC rings behind an atomic fanout (offset-0 events, works while stopped, drop-newest + flush-on-drop so notes can't stick); recording shares the audio take's `PlaybackAnchor` beat math and lands MIDI clips in the same "Record Take N" undo step (punch trims audio only, v0); `midi.listInputs` + snapshot `midiInputs`/`midiEventCount` + MCP; hardware-free verification via virtual sources (`scripts/e2e-midi-record.swift`)

## M4 — Mixer & FX suite
- [x] (i) Bus routing & sends — bus tracks become real mix destinations: track output routing (master or bus), per-track sends (pre/post fader, level) into buses, bus volume/pan/mute/solo/meters in the engine graph + offline render parity; `track.setOutput` / `track.addSend|setSend|removeSend` commands + MCP + tests — **COMPLETE 2026-07-06**: the parked mid-play send-activation defect resolved (stale render-clock anchor after the rewire bounce, one-flag fix — see the RESOLVED entry under ## Blocked); live bus E2E 19/19 (a send added mid-play meters on its bus with NO transport command: within ~0.3 s for audio material, at the next note-on/loop wrap for MIDI material per the settled no-chase contract)
- [x] (ii) FX insert-chain architecture — `EffectRendering` seam + `ChainHostAU` (in-process registered AU `aufx/dwch/DAWP`, spike-proven from the SPM binary) as a permanent per-strip insert point; chain edits are atomically published snapshots, NEVER graph mutations (the M4 (i) failure class eliminated by construction — verified live: mid-play add/bypass/reorder/param with zero transport interruption); pre-fader inserts on audio/instrument/bus strips; `fx.add|remove|reorder|setBypass|setParam|describe` + 6 MCP tools; GainEffect proves the seam (bit-exact chain math); PDC-ready latency reporting (0 in v0)
- [x] (iii) Built-in FX pack 1 (dynamics & tone) — 4-band parametric EQ (RBJ biquads, TDF2 Float64, 0 dB bands bit-exact-skipped), soft-knee stereo-linked compressor (real peak detector, log-domain gain computer), 5 ms-lookahead brickwall limiter (monotonic-deque window max; `|out| ≤ ceiling` for EVERY sample by construction; first nonzero `latencySamples`, plumbed engine→store→wire per-effect); spectrum display deferred to mixer UI (vi)
- [x] (iv) Built-in FX pack 2 (space & color) — Freeverb reverb (8 damped combs + 4 allpasses/channel, pre-delay, mid/side width), stereo delay (integer lines, echo at exactly round(timeMs×rate/1000), feedback high-cut, ping-pong crossfeed), tanh saturator (−driveDb/2 dB fixed compensation, odd harmonics at H2/H3≈1e-15), stereo-linked gate (bit-exact passthrough open, true zeros closed, attack/hold/release ramps), 2-voice chorus (90° stereo / 180° inter-voice sine LFO offsets); all atomic-POD-publish RT-safe; 9 kinds on `fx.describe` + MCP; verified live (delay echoes in bounce, mid-play reverb add, −1 dB gate silence, 5-kind persistence)
- [x] (v) AU effect hosting (headless) — `HostedAUEffect` pull-render adapter behind `EffectRendering` (in-place via stash box + preallocated scratch ABL, render failure → dry passthrough); registry reuses the instrument timeout-raced prepare with strict-select on add / placeholder+warning on load; async prepare → single-strip invalidate → chain republish (never a graph rebuild); real `latencySamples` from `au.latency` (AUPeakLimiter: 96 @ 48 kHz); fullState in `.dawproj`; `fx.add kind=audioUnit` + `fx.listAudioUnits` + MCP (47 tools); found+fixed AUv2-bridge input-bus-disabled bug; plugin UI windows stay gated on M9 bundling
- [x] (vi) Full mixer view — console-style horizontal rack: channel strips (kind badge, inserts w/ bypass dots, sends w/ mini-faders, output picker, pan knob, long-throw fader w/ dB readout, mute/solo/arm glow buttons, live meter), BUSES-divided bus strips, accent-bordered pinned MASTER w/ stereo meter; `MixerModel` headless view-model in DAWAppKit (strip derivation/dB/pan/geometry, 10 tests); ARRANGE⇄MIX header toggle + `ui.showMixer` debug-tier command; Canvas controls (VerticalFader/PanKnob/SendMiniFader); design language codified as the "Mixer console" pattern in DESIGN-LANGUAGE.md; verified by captureUI self-render (agent + independent) — master insert chain deferred until DAWCore grows a master effect chain; hosted-AU insert picker UI deferred (control-plane add works)
- [x] (vii-a) Automation domain model + store CRUD — `AutomationLane/Target/Point/Curve` in DAWCore (target enum persistence-stable incl. deferred sendLevel; pure `value(atBeat:)` evaluator, canonicalized points, clamp-through-target-range), `Track.automation` additive Codable, ProjectStore CRUD (idempotent addLane w/ v0 rejections, whole-array setPoints w/ 4096 cap + `automation.points:<laneID>` coalescing, cascades drop targeting lanes inside removeEffect/removeSend/bus-delete edits), `.dawproj` persistence omitted-when-empty (pre-automation saves byte-identical); 19 tests

  - [x] (crash-a) Teardown crash: root-cause + fix + regression guard — **FIXED 2026-07-10 (root cause PROVEN with a 6-experiment live evidence matrix, not conjectured)**: mechanism = AVFoundation completes a node detach's internal graph bookkeeping ONLY when it can synchronize with a running engine; detaching a once-rendered node while STOPPED leaves a stale raw `AUGraphNodeBaseV3*` in `AVAudioEngineGraph`'s internal list (survives `reset()`+`start()`; explicit disconnect-first does NOT purge it — experiments D1/D2), ARC then frees the node, and the next live `connect` (first track.add after project.new) walks freed memory. Our teardown hit it because reconcile's removal pass fires the quiesce-STOP at the first non-trivial strip, so the mass detach ran stopped-after-running. KEY INVERSION (exp C): stop-before-any-detach — the "cleanest" reading of the old discipline — still crashes; the discipline's stop IS the poison for detaches (safe direction is attach-while-stopped/cold-start; detach needs RUNNING — exp B/E clean incl. a full 28-strip live teardown). H2 refuted (every removed node had a paired detach); H3 not the trigger but the fix restores the detach-sync guarantee the renderer-deinit WATCH item leans on. FIX (minimal): `PlaybackGraph.retireNode(_:)`/`flushRetiredNodes()` + `engineHasRun` — running or never-ran → detach now (offline/test byte-identical); stopped-after-running → park attached+strongly-held in a FIFO pending-detach bin (no freed-memory window CAN exist), flushed after every successful `engine.start()` (prepare/rewire-bounce/config-recovery) + defensively at reconcile entry; every teardown detach site seam-routed incl. dead `detachAll()`; announce/bounce semantics untouched; silently covers latent same-class windows (live addSend old-gain teardown, invalidateInstrumentNode, mixed bus-removal passes). - [x] (m10-h) Piano roll time-range editing — **COMPLETE 2026-07-11** (swift-app-engineer + mcp-integration-engineer, first wire-surface growth of M10): DAWCore `deleteTimeRange`/`insertTimeRange` on ProjectStore — clip-LOCAL beats, MIDI-only, ONE `performEdit` each (single undo step). DOCUMENTED SPLICE SEMANTICS: delete excises `[start, start+len)` + closes the gap — wholly-before unchanged, crossing-from-before keeps its outside parts (ends-inside → trimmed to the cut; spans-whole-range → head+tail rejoin), onset-inside REMOVED, at/after shifts left; clip shrinks, floored at 1 beat / furthest note end. Insert shifts `startBeat >= atBeat` right; a crossing note keeps start AND length (sustains across the gap, never split); `atBeat == clipLength` appends. Wire: `clip.deleteTimeRange {clipId,startBeat,lengthBeats}` / `clip.insertTimeRange {clipId,atBeat,lengthBeats}` → updated clip encoding (allCommands 102→**104**); MCP `clip_delete_time_range`/`clip_insert_time_range` (105→**107** tools; suite-bijection-derived names); CopilotCatalog clip section 11; ARCHITECTURE.md table updated.   **MEASURED 2026-07-27 (user proposed importing already-trained RVC models instead of training): THE DROP-IN PATH ALREADY EXISTS IN THE FACADE.** `_scan_voices()` (`scripts/rvc/server.py:168-176`) is a **plain directory scan** of `runtime/voices/<voiceId>/` — no registration, no training call. `_voice_descriptor` (`:145-165`) reports `ready` when `model.npz` is present, **`needsConversion` when only `model.pth` is** (the MLX runtime cannot consume raw `.pth`), `incomplete` otherwise; `model.index` and `voice.json` are optional. **So a trained voice can be added by copying a folder** — `/v1/voice/train`'s 501 does NOT block this, and `/v1/voice/convert` (`:249`) is real. **The one real gap is a `.pth` → `.npz` conversion step for user-supplied voices** (precedent exists: the installer derives `base_f0G40k.npz` and `rmvpe_mlx.npz` via the fork's own tools). **POLICY REVERSED BY THE USER 2026-07-27 — the own-voice-only restriction is WITHDRAWN.** Their words: *"We need to support third-party voice models and let user deal with repercussions if they use some celebrity vocals without approval, our model should be open and not restrict."* This supersedes the earlier own-voice-only policy recorded from 2026-07-11 (m10-p-1 "NO celebrity voice models — base/demo checkpoints only") and every restatement of it. **DAW Pro imports whatever voice model the user supplies; provenance and licensing are the user's responsibility, not the app's.** Consequences to carry forward: (1) the voice-store comment at `scripts/rvc/server.py:138-141` ("Voices are ONLY ever created by training on the user's own recordings") is now STALE and should be corrected when that file is next touched; (2) the Voice panel's own-voice-only copy from m10-p-5 ("a voice you have the rights to use") needs a rewrite — **recommended, not mandated: keep a plain one-line notice rather than a gate**, since it costs nothing and puts the responsibility where the user just placed it; (3) the import path needs no provenance check of any kind. - [x] (m11-d) Arrange-level clip crossfades + overlap correctness — **COMPLETE 2026-07-11**. Fixes the audit's NEW-DISCOVERY BUG (`ProjectStore.moveClip` did no overlap detection → overlapped ordinary clips silently volume-doubled with no crossfade/error) AND ships a crossfade tool. **Policy (settled, no render change): §1** no SILENT overlap of ordinary same-track clips — only the explicit crossfade tool creates a legitimate overlap (covered by complementary fades); **§2** a move that lands on a stationary same-track clip TRIMS the covered region (full containment → the stationary clip is removed), audio+MIDI alike, comp members exempt, all inside ONE "Move Clip" undo; **§3** m10-k targeted-track import applies the same trim inside its single-undo batch (new-track placements unaffected); **§4** moving a previously-crossfaded clip re-trims against current boundaries, fades survive as plain per-clip fades. **DAWCore**: value types `ClipMoveResult {clip, trimmedClipIDs, removedClipIDs}` + `CrossfadeResult {left, right, overlapBeats}`; `moveClip` rewritten to rebuild the lane applying `resolveOverlap` to each ordinary (non-comp-member) same-track clip; statics `clipTrimmedToWindow` (mirrors `trimClip`: advances `startOffsetSeconds`, drops/truncates MIDI, clamps fades; nil below `minClipLengthBeats`) + `resolveOverlap` (disjoint→keep / full-cover→drop / head-cover→trim leading / tail-or-inside→trim trailing); `crossfadeClips(trackId, clipId, otherClipId, lengthBeats)` — AUDIO only, adjacent or overlapping ≤ length, symmetric half-each extension where source material exists (else field-named `crossfadeNeedsMaterial`), legacy-overlap normalization, complementary `equalPower` fades spanning EXACTLY the final overlap, each clip's OTHER fade preserved, ONE "Crossfade Clips" undo; `importAudioBatch` existing-track path now applies `resolveOverlap`. **Wire (+1, 114→115)**: `clip.crossfade {trackId, clipId, otherClipId, lengthBeats>0}` → `{left, right, overlapBeats}` (field-named errors `crossfadeNotEligible`/`crossfadeNeedsMaterial`); `clip.move` response gained additive `trimmed:[UUID]`/`removed:[UUID]` (existing `clip` fields byte-identical). **MCP (+1, 117→118)**: `clip_crossfade` teaching the split→butt→crossfade workflow; `clip_move` description carries the overlap policy + the additive fields. **Copilot catalog 43→44** (clip 11→12; `clip.crossfade` added, non-destructive-beyond-undo). **UI**: arrange clip context menu **"Crossfade with Next"** (¼/½/1/2-beat submenu, audio-only, only when a crossfadeable next clip exists) → `ProjectStore.crossfadeClips`; a green **X-bowtie seam badge** over each overlapping ordinary audio pair (the clips' own fade wedges shade the overlap — neutral audio green, never violet); `.crossfade` Explain entry (catalog **61→62**). **Explain overlay robustness (thin-view fix, general)**: `ExplainOverlay.cardCenter` now clamps the card center in BOTH axes so a card flipping above a high anchor (e.g. the seam badge near the ruler) can no longer clip its title off the window top. `debug.explainMode {focus:"crossfade"}` stages the capture. **GATE (staging 17695, headless WS driver)**: suite **1908/222** (+ `DAWCoreTests.ClipCrossfadeTests` 13, `DAWControlTests.ClipCrossfadeCommandTests` 7), 0 warnings incl. `--build-tests`; npm **84/84** (0 skipped, 118-tool pin exercised); live gate ALL PASS — **(a) doubling fixed, MEASURED**: single clip **−10.66 LUFS**, a second identical clip stacked on top **−4.64 LUFS** (the pre-fix bug reproduced, **+6.02 dB**), then a 50 %-overlap move trimmed the stationary clip → **−10.63 LUFS** (**afterVsSingle 0.03 dB — doubling gone**), `clip.move` returned `trimmed:[clipA]`; **(b) crossfade proof**: `overlapBeats 1`, both fades `equalPower` == overlap, windowed continuity across two DECORRELATED noise clips **−10.66 / −10.67 / −10.69 LUFS** (**seam spread 0.03 dB** — seamless), `edit.history` top **"Crossfade Clips"** (ONE step) and undo reverted the fade, needs-material error field-named ("clip 'noiseA' has no audio before its start…"); **(c) import overlap**: `debug.importAudio` onto a resident clip → 2 clips, resident trimmed 8→**4 beats**, **no overlap**, one batch undo "Import 'noiseA'". Pixel-reviewed captures: the crossfaded seam with the X-bowtie + crossing fade wedges, and the `.crossfade` Explain card. **DEVIATIONS**: (1) the "Crossfade with Next" **context menu is not captured** — `debug.captureUI` uses `cacheDisplay`, which cannot render an AppKit `NSMenu` popup (the menu item + Explain copy stand in as evidence); (2) equal-power crossfades hold constant POWER only for DECORRELATED material — identical/correlated material sums coherently (+3 dB), so gate (b) uses two independent noise files, not a split of one file (documented in the gate + command doc). Wire surface now **115 cmds / 118 tools**, Copilot catalog **44**, Explain **62**. **UPDATE (orchestrator verification):** independent suite re-runs green — Swift 1908/222, npm 84/84 explicit (0 skipped); counts re-verified (allCommands 115, registerTool 118, ExplainID 62, catalog carries clip.crossfade); all three captures pixel-reviewed (seam bowtie + crossing wedges, fully-rendered Explain card); OWN independently-authored render-and-measure gate on a DIFFERENT scenario (0.5-beat crossfade vs the agent's 1-beat; trim-then-move-adjacent construction) passed ALL checks — post-trim span LUFS **identical** to the single-clip reference (−10.6288 both), `clip.move` reported `trimmed:[…]`, overlap exactly 0.5 with equalPower fades == overlap, seam spread **0.03 dB** across decorrelated noise, one-step "Crossfade Clips" undo reverting the fades, needs-material error verbatim (scratchpad verify-m11d-xfade.mjs). Bonus live proof: my first script draft imported B overlapping the trimmed A and the IMPORT policy correctly trimmed the resident clip — which broke my adjacency math and the crossfade rightly refused with the teaching error; the "failure" was the policy working.
- [x] (m11-e) Track bounce-in-place — **COMPLETE 2026-07-11**. Render-and-land vertical over `ProjectStore.bounceTrackInPlace(trackId, fromBeat?, durationSeconds?, muteSource?, name?)` (`Sources/DAWCore/ProjectStore+Bounce.swift`), reusing `render.stems`' render machinery (full-session forced PDC, single post-fader `StemPlan.passTracks` pass, same default window, NEVER normalized — DAWEngine untouched) and `importGeneration`'s land discipline (render+write OUTSIDE the edit body into the project-stable `generationImportsDirectory`; ONE `performEdit("Bounce in Place")` inserts the `<source> (Bounced)` audio track+clip DIRECTLY AFTER the source and mutes the source when `muteSource` default-true). Eligibility is IDENTICAL to `render.stems` for one track (`StemPlan.descriptors`): bus-routed source → `stemNotMasterInput` verbatim, unknown → `trackNotFound`, empty window → `nothingToRender`; a bus bounces. Undo removes the new track+clip AND restores the source's prior mute; the rendered FILE stays on disk (import semantics). `renderCompletedCount` deliberately NOT ticked (that's the export-song onboarding signal). Result `BounceInPlaceResult {track, clip, file, sourceTrackId, sourceMuted, measurement}`. **Wire (+1, 115→116)**: `track.bounceInPlace`. **MCP (+1, 118→119)**: `track_bounce_in_place`; NOT in the Copilot catalog (long-running render op — the `render.*` exclusion precedent, documented). **UI**: track-header context menu "Bounce in Place" (master-input tracks only, both densities — no density split, no inventory row); Explain `.bounceInPlace` (catalog 62→**63**). Ship: collision-free deterministic ids in DAWCore, a uniqueness test across ALL built-ins, and an audit of id-keyed consumers; the ids are deterministic and not persisted in projects, so a one-time wire-visible id change is safe — note it in the `groove.list` command doc and CHANGELOG. Bug was WORSE than triaged: the single-pass byte fold collapsed the ENTIRE `swing8:54…75` family (all 22 percentages) onto ONE UUID `48C63252-293C-B111-D60F-9903BFCCE689` — only 23 distinct ids among the 44 resolvable names (red run recorded). Fix (Groove.swift:135–174): two independent 64-bit FNV-1a passes over distinctly-salted strings (`groove.builtin.hi:`/`.lo:`) fill the two 8-byte halves + RFC-4122 v4 version/variant bits (cosmetic); pure, deterministic, 44/44 distinct. +4 tests (uniqueness over 44, determinism, stability pin `swing8:66` = `0C5B2E4D-148B-4FA9-B8A1-31A0C07182EF`, collided-pairs-differ) → **1931/224**. Saved-template ids (random `UUID()` at extract, ProjectStore+Quantize.swift:241) provably untouched. `groove.list` doc-comment notes the one-time id change. Wire/MCP counts HELD 116/119; npm untouched. not in the audit's four). **AUDITED-BUT-EXEMPT, per-verb reasoning:** `splitClip` (the two halves TILE the original window — no new footprint); `setClipStretch`/`setClipFades`/`setClipGain`/`setClipNotes` (no start/length change — same footprint); `deleteTimeRange` (only SHRINKS a clip); bounce-in-place + `importGeneration`/`importGeneratedStems` (always land on a NEW track — nothing to overlap); `fixClipRegion`/`importClipFix` + the take subsystem `groupTakes`/`flattenTakeGroup`/`moveTakeGroup`/`rebuildCompMembers` (take domain — the comp projection is non-overlapping BY CONSTRUCTION and takes intentionally stack — exempt by policy); audio-quantize `applyAudioQuantize` (slice placements clamp to `[clipStart+minSlice, clipEnd-minSlice]`, head=clipStart, tail=clipEnd → the slice UNION ⊆ the original clip footprint, so it can NEVER extend past it); recording take-landing (audio auto-groups on overlap; MIDI re-recording STACKS per the documented v0 policy — the take-stacking domain, m13-c owns recording-time concerns). **UNIT TESTS +15 / +1 suite** (`NoSilentOverlapCompletionTests`, appended to Tests/DAWCoreTests/ClipCrossfadeTests.swift beside m11-d's suite): per bypass path — geometry/resident-trim, single-undo restores BOTH clips, sub-`minClipLength` remnant removal — across trim/stretch/addAudio/addMIDI, plus the addAudio FULL-doubling worst-shape collapse-to-single, the `insertTimeRange` growth+undo, and a REGRESSION PIN that take-group members STILL STACK under an overlapping import. I ran an INVALID stash A/B (HEAD 0ad2e51 is months old — M10…m13-d are all uncommitted, so a stash isolates nothing) and wrongly concluded the drift was pre-existing — the coordinator STOPPED me. Root cause was NOT a code regression: an earlier g3 run's `track.bounceInPlace` had been AUTOSAVED into the canonical m12-b `project.json` (savedAt 22:01:50Z), adding a "Keys (Bounced)" track + muting "Keys" — test-harness contamination. FIX: restored the pristine project (removed bounced track, un-muted Keys, deleted bounce media), took a gate COPY at `m13e/m12b-gate.dawproj` so the gate's own bounce can't re-contaminate the bundle, and restored the ORIGINAL law anchors — which then reproduced all five SHAs exactly. **G5** live wire gate (staging :17695) **17/17** — set echoes/sorts points, snapshot carries gainEnvelope + omits-when-empty, unsorted+duplicate+MIDI errors verbatim, rapid burst = ONE undo entry ("Set Clip Gain Envelope") landing the final value, ONE undo reverts the whole gesture, >800 ms gap = TWO entries, split keeps both halves + seam continuity, trim survives re-window. **G6** two SHA-distinct captures (Pro: cyan envelope polyline + breakpoint dots at beat 6/−18 dB dip & beat 12/+6 dB swell over selected audio clip; Simple: same clip + waveform, overlay HIDDEN) — pixel-reviewed. **G7** 0 build warnings; Swift **2090/243 → 2111/245** (+21 tests / +2 suites, incl. the 3 review-defect regressions below); npm **86/86** (tempo-lint held). **Change surface:** DAWCore (Model.swift, ProjectStore.swift, ProjectDocument.swift), DAWEngine (ClipFadeBake.swift, PlaybackGraph.swift), DAWControl (Commands.swift, CopilotCatalog.swift), DAWAppKit (ExplainModel.swift), DAWApp (Timeline/TimelineLanesView.swift, ContentView.swift), mcp-server (src/server.ts, test/integration.test.ts), tests (ClipGainEnvelopeTests.swift NEW, ClipEnvelopeRenderTests.swift, ControlTests.swift exclusion, SoundBankCommandTests.swift pin, CopilotCatalogTests.swift pin). Staging torn down per law (killed by exact pid, `.session.lock` cleared, `.ips` count 20→20 — zero new crash reports). Artifacts in scratchpad/m13e/ (g3-result.json, g5-result.json, g6-pro-envelope.png / g6-simple-hidden.png, m12b-gate.dawproj, ips-before/after.txt). **POST-REVIEW (audio-dsp-engineer bake-path review + orchestrator wire gate): 2 real adjacent-path defects FIXED, 2 alleged render/bounce defects PROVEN to be gate-script bugs (code correct).** **D1 (FIXED) — take-group flatten dropped the envelope:** `CompFlattener.flatten` (Takes.swift) copied gainDb/stretch/pitch but omitted `gainEnvelope`, so recording a 2nd take / landing a ClipFix onto an enveloped clip silently muted the envelope (and comp members reject `setClipGainEnvelope` → unrecoverable). Fix = window it with the arrange split/trim discipline `Clip.windowedGainEnvelope(source.gainEnvelope, delta: memberStart − laneStart, newLength: memberLength)`. Test `gainEnvelopeSurvivesFlatten` (TakeCompTests) pins the windowed V-envelope on the materialized member. **D2 (FIXED) — ClipFix staleness fingerprint ignored the envelope:** `ClipGeometryFingerprint` (ClipFix.swift) checked gainDb but not `gainEnvelope`; the submit-time dry copy baked the OLD envelope, so an envelope edit mid-job slipped through as a "pure move" while an equivalent gainDb edit threw `clipFixStale`. Fix = add `gainEnvelope` to the fingerprint + `moveDelta` guard + the stale-message (`"gain envelope (N → M points)"`). Test `staleGainEnvelopeEditMidJob` (ClipFixTests). **D3 (NO CODE BUG — gate-script defect) — "non-constant envelope absent in mixdown":** the render IS correct; the repro script `verify-m13e-orch.mjs::wavSamples` read the STEREO WAV interleaved-flat without deinterleaving, so its "beat 6" index 141600 actually sampled frame 70800 = beat 2.95 (the flat 0 dB head), giving 0.0 delta; the constant control passed only because a uniform scale is index-invariant. PROVEN by (a) in-memory `channelData[0]` (already deinterleaved) RMS: A2 window −6.00 dB, A4 −10.5 dB exactly; (b) deinterleaving disk `a-ramp.wav` ch0: beat 6 = −5.9965 dB, beat 7.5 = −10.469 dB, head 0.0. Regression `nonConstantEnvelopeAppliesOnReRender` (ClipEnvelopeRenderTests) renders the SAME clip dry-THEN-enveloped (the wire's ordering, MONO 48 k PCM16 source) and asserts the ramp applies on the re-render — closing the "stale envelope-blind bake cache" hypothesis (there is no bake cache; OfflineRenderer builds a fresh graph per render). **D4 (NO CODE BUG — gate-script defect) — "bounce retains envelope → double-apply":** `bounceTrackInPlace` (ProjectStore+Bounce.swift:133) already lands a FRESH `Clip(...)` with an EMPTY envelope on a NEW "(Bounced)" track pointing to the baked stem file; the gate's B2 read `clipsOf(doc, sourceTrackId)` — the SOURCE track (correctly muted, correctly retaining its own envelope), not the bounced track. Verified live: bounced track clip `gainEnvelope=[]`, and deinterleaved b-post == b-pre at every beat (no double-apply). Staging re-swept per law (.session.lock cleared, .ips 20→20). NEXT: M13 remaining (m13-f gapless-loop spike, m13-g AU picker + ruler pinning, m13-h meter-aware snap, m13-i docs). — **ORCHESTRATOR UPDATE (independent verification, 2026-07-12): CONFIRMED first-hand → m13-e CLOSED.** Re-ran myself: `swift build --build-tests` **0 warnings**, Swift **2111/245** pass, npm **86/86**; count pins re-greped (wire **120**, MCP **123** via registerTool regex, catalog **52** + pin, `.clipGainEnvelope` at ExplainModel.swift:104/:339 = Explain 67). Both G6 captures personally pixel-reviewed (Pro: cyan polyline + breakpoint dots at distinct gains + 0.0 dB drag readout on the selected clip; Simple: overlay fully hidden, waveform intact — density honesty; UX NIT for m13-g: the Pro overlay hides the waveform under it, points would be easier to place with the waveform ghosted). **MANDATED audio-dsp REVIEW ran (read-only): RT-safety PASS (zero envelope symbols reachable from render surfaces; bake main-actor only), numerics PASS (dB-linear evaluator, static gain never double-applied, empty-envelope guard preserves legacy arithmetic), invalidation PASS (ClipKey compare at reconcile frequency), tempo-map PASS (m12-c integral machinery)** — plus TWO REAL DEFECTS (D1 flatten drop / D2 ClipFix fingerprint, both fixed above with regressions) and a PERF FOLLOW-UP (filed as m13-h2 below). **MY OWN live gate (verify-m13e-orch.mjs, scratchpad/m13e — scenarios neither G2 nor G5 covered): 22/22 PASS** on staging :17695 — (A) envelope changes RENDERED AUDIO over the wire: constant −6.0206 dB exact (±0.01), ramp midpoint beat 6 = −6 dB and tail beats 7.2-7.8 = −10.5 dB analytic, flat head byte-clean; (B) bounce-in-place lands the clip on the "(Bounced)" track with EMPTY envelope + source muted + post==pre mixdown (no double-apply); (C) m13-b interplay: overlap-import trims the enveloped resident AND re-windows the envelope with an exactly-interpolated boundary point (−12@4); (D) clip.move keeps envelope beats clip-relative; (E) gainDb −200 clamped, out-of-range beat handled; (F) undo/redo storm incl. undo-of-clear restoring the 2-point envelope; (G) **8th-era anchor proof, strongest form yet: pristine-copy → OPEN → SAVE → REOPEN through the new binary → default mixdown SHA == b4c56164… AND window == a14f4615… EXACT, saved JSON carries zero gainEnvelope keys**. `.ips` 20→20 across every session today. **DEFECT LEDGER (honest accounting):** review found 2 real (D1/D2, fixed+pinned); my gate alleged 2 more (D3 ramp-no-op / D4 bounce-retains) that the agent DISPROVED empirically — both were MY script's bugs (stereo WAV read without deinterleaving mis-positioned windowed RMS; bounce semantics misread) — and it still added the dry-then-enveloped ordering regression as permanent hardening. GATE LAWS learned: `clip.move` takes `toStartBeat`; windowed WAV analysis MUST deinterleave (whole-file RMS masks it); snapshot JSON key order is nondeterministic — compare envelope by VALUE never stringify; anchor mixes are DEFAULT-parameterized. - [x] (m13-h2) **Envelope-bake perf follow-up** (filed from the m13-e audio-dsp review — correctness is NOT at stake, the null/analytic gates all pass): a non-empty gain envelope currently converts the clip's bake plan to ONE full-region baked piece, so `bakePiece` (PlaybackGraph.swift ~:1710) allocates and per-frame-evaluates the entire scheduled region on the main actor at EVERY `scheduleAll` (play/seek/loop restart) — ~230 MB + ~29 M `envelopeGain` calls (per-frame `pow` + O(points) scan) for a 10-min stereo 48 k clip. Candidate fixes (pick via measurement, audio-dsp-engineer): piecewise bake only the inter-point spans whose gain ≠ unity (mirror the fade three-piece plan), hoist the dB→linear `pow` to per-ramp incremental multiplication, and/or cache the baked buffer keyed by (file, shape, envelope) across scheduleAll calls. Also inherits the reviewer's note that the pre-existing bake-failure catch fallback (PlaybackGraph.swift ~:1727-1734) now silently drops the envelope too ("timing wins", stderr-logged) — decide if that deserves a user-visible warning. GATE: perf measurement before/after on a long enveloped clip (bake time + peak RSS at scheduleAll), null-case byte gate era, analytic envelope render tests unchanged. — **COMPLETE 2026-07-12 (audio-dsp-engineer).** Fix chosen BY MEASUREMENT: **piecewise bake** (`ClipFadeBake.envelopedPiecePlan` + `PlaybackGraph.scheduleEnvelopedPieces`) — only spans the envelope/fades actually shape are baked (±1 guard frame, streamed gaps < 8_192 frames fold into the bake); provably-unity spans stream straight off the file, bit-identical to the old `× 1.0` multiply, so rendered bytes are UNCHANGED in every case (not just null). MEASURED (release, 10-min stereo 48 k clip, 4-beat dip, `OfflineRenderer` 0.25 s pull ≡ one scheduleAll): mostly-unity **191.5 ms → 40.6 ms** median (dry floor 38.8 ms; bake cost ~153 ms → ~1.5 ms), peak RSS **287 MB → 20 MB** (dry 18 MB); flat-0 dB envelope 203 → 39.2 ms (zero bake); full-non-unity stays ~205 ms/284 MB (envelope genuinely shapes everything — honest cost; pow-hoist + cache REJECTED: read+alloc dominates, numerics/invalidation risk buys nothing in the common case). Byte gates all four SHA-identical before/after (null `0adcccbd…`, enveloped `83f2e4c1…`, mid-seek `3d939bf3…`, Bus Song bundle COPY `1d9e5e3f…`); m13-e analytic/null tests pass UNMODIFIED (unity-envelope residue now exactly 0.0). Fallback decision: bake failure stays stderr-only (clip still sounds on time; failure = transient second-reader I/O on a file the player already opened) but the message now says explicitly it drops envelope AND fades; follow-up to file = engine-notices surface for schedule-time degradations. New `ClipEnvelopeBakePlanTests` (10 tests: plan-shape pins incl. mostly-unity ≤ 96_008 baked frames vs 28.8 M region, flat-unity single streamed piece, sliver-fold, partition sweep, streamed-frames-exactly-unity law under applyEnvelope's own frame→beat mapping incl. multi-segment map, streamed-spans-bit-exact render, mid-seek, determinism). Suite 2134/247 → **2144/248**; wire/MCP untouched (120/123 hold).

Order: a → b → c (correctness; independent of each other) → d → e (features) → f (spike) → g → h → i (debt/docs). Audit takeaway worth pinning: "the biggest wins were things the wire already did that no human could reach, and the worst bugs were on the newest and the oldest seams, not the middle."

## M14 — Gapless loop wrap (seeded 2026-07-12 from the m13-f design spike: docs/research/design-m13f-gapless-loop.md, GO-WITH-CONDITIONS; implementation follows that doc's §9 phases L-1…L-4 and §10 conditions C1–C8 verbatim — deviations return to the design doc for amendment)

**Standing law (C1)**: `GaplessLoopSpikeTests` (the L-0 splice-primitive pins, 4 tests) stays green forever; any splice regression blocks M14 work that day and converts the milestone to NO-GO/park with the spike retained as evidence (m10-p precedent). **Standing law (C4)**: zero new RT surface beyond the bounded cursor re-seek — no allocation, locks, or ObjC on the render path in ANY phase; any deviation returns to the design doc for amendment. Offline/render parity (§7): nothing changes offline, ever — provably (C8).

- [x] (m14-a / L-1) **Transport core** — AudioEngine + PlaybackGraph, control thread only: loop window in `scheduleAll`, per-cycle unrolled audio segments + baked-buffer cache, `derivedBeats` modular branch + timeline law, playhead-task top-up (`scheduledThroughCycle`), the wrap path stops calling `restart` (the graph never stops at wrap). Route: audio-dsp-engineer (design §2, §4 Option A, §8 failure modes 2–4). ## M15 — Post-gapless correctness & agent-ergonomics round (seeded 2026-07-13 from the post-M14 audit: docs/research/audit-m15.md, 12 findings F1–F12, 2 measured live bugs; orchestrator spot-checked the F1 both-call-sites citation, the F8 dismissal evidence, and the F5 mechanism before authoring) — **MILESTONE COMPLETE 2026-07-13 (a–g all verified)**

- [x] (m15-a) **Metronome meter-map correctness — the audit's HIGH measured bug (F1).** The live click ignores meter changes: with 4/4→3/4 at beat 8, downbeats land at 0,4,8,12,16,20 (constant 4/4) instead of 0,4,8,11,14,17,20 — measured via debug.masterCapture amplitude+frequency classification of all 23 clicks. Mechanism: `AudioEngine.clickMeterMap` builds `MeterMap(constant:)` from the cached `beatsPerBar` scalar (AudioEngine.swift:1607-1609; cache :1478) — and `OfflineRenderer.swift:246` does the SAME (its in-code claim "offline callers pass real maps" is FALSE — only tests pass real maps); count-in inherits the scalar too (wrong bar length in a non-base meter, code-proven). Fix: plumb the REAL `store.transport.meterMap` through both call sites + count-in; the Metronome API is already fully MeterMap-aware (m14-c) so this is plumbing, S-sized, control-plane only. Route: audio-dsp-engineer. GATE: the audit's capture probe re-run verbatim — live 4/4→3/4 downbeats at 0,4,8,11,14,17,20 by amplitude+frequency classification; offline render click positions == the meter map; count-in bar length correct in 3/4; C8-style null era (constant-meter project byte-identical); the m14-c meter pin extended to the LIVE path. — **COMPLETE 2026-07-13, ALL GATES PASS** (audio-dsp-engineer). Production surface = 4 files. AudioEngine.swift: the cached `beatsPerBar` scalar is GONE from the engine (it existed solely for the click map + count-in) — `cacheTransportFlags` caches the WHOLE `transport.meterMap` as the stored `clickMeterMap` (a trivial project synthesizes the constant map, so the null case is arithmetic-identical), the computed constant-map property and its false comment DELETED, all four live schedule sites read the cache, count-in derives bar length from it. Metronome.swift: `countInPlan`/`scheduleCountIn` take `meterMap:` — bar length = the meter AT THE RECORD BEAT (the m12-b tempo LOOKUP policy's meter twin: count-in precedes the record beat in wall time, the beat domain does not extend backward) + `receivedMeterMap` test seam. OfflineRenderer.swift: `render(beatsPerBar:)` → `render(meterMap:)` (default trivial 4/4). ProjectStore.swift — **DISCOVERY: a meter-ONLY map change never reached the engine at all** (`applyTempoMap` pushed only on tempo change; `restoreEditState` likewise), which would have left the new cache stale mid-play: both funnels now push the click-player-local `metronomeChanged` intent on a meter-only change (transport/clip players untouched; tempo changes need nothing extra — setTempo's restart re-caches + reschedules); stale "meter needs no engine push" comments corrected. EVIDENCE — the audit probe VERBATIM (headless staging :17641, project.new → `tempo.setMap {segments, meterChanges}` → setMetronome → debug.masterCapture 552 000 frames): 23/23 clicks classified by amplitude AND frequency, zero ambiguity — downbeats 0,4,8,11,14,17,20 EXACT (0.483 @ 1609 Hz vs 0.333 @ 1000 Hz); the bug pattern 0,4,8,12,16,20 dead; app killed by exact pid, port verified closed, .ips listings byte-equal. PROBE QUIRK for the next agent: `debug.masterCapture start` REQUIRES a running engine — warm-up play/stop first (`stopPlayback` leaves the hardware engine running). OFFLINE == MAP: the same scenario through OfflineRenderer, all 23 clicks classified, downbeats == the map — the m14-c meter pin now covers the offline PATH, not just the API. COUNT-IN: unit pins (constant 3/4 ⇒ 3 clicks; audit map ⇒ 3 at beat 9, 4 at beat 0, 3 at beat 8 — right-continuous) + a rendered `scheduleCountIn` pin (2 bars at beat 9 ⇒ 6 clicks, downbeats on clicks 0/3, silence after). PLUMBING CHAIN pinned at both links: intents cache `transport.meterMap` ITSELF (headless pin) + live smoke — all FOUR live schedule paths hand the Metronome the real 2-change map verbatim. NULL ERA: new permanent 4/4-only anchor pair OFF `a3927faf…` / ON `7dd272aa…` byte-identical before/after the change (the OFF SHA coincides with the m14-c fixture's because that fixture's 120→90 boundary sits past all its clip content — noted in the suite header); the m14-c era pair OFF `a3927faf…` / ON `692e713d…` HELD through the only test call-shape changes (`beatsPerBar: 3` → the equivalent constant map, 3 sites, zero expectation changes). 2168/253 → **2173/254** (+5 tests, +1 suite MetronomeMeterMapTests), npm 86/86, wire **120/123 HELD** (DAWControl/mcp-server mtimes pre-date the session), build warning-clean, C4 zero new RT surface (control-thread scheduling only).

- [ ] (m17-i) **MLX-native ACE-Step spike (research first; user asked 2026-07-16 whether the model can be embedded natively via Apple Neural Engine/MLX instead of the external sidecar).** Position recorded: ANE (Core ML) is NOT viable for this model class (multi-billion-param diffusion transformer; ANE wants small static-shape fp16 nets) — the plausible native path is MLX on the GPU. IN-PROCESS embedding stays REJECTED regardless of framework: the main process owns the RT audio engine, a model spiking tens of GB of unified memory + GPU contention in-process risks dropouts, and a model crash must never be a DAW crash (M16 lesson) — heavy AI belongs out-of-process. The REAL candidate: replace the Python/FastAPI sidecar with a NATIVE MLX helper process (mlx-swift, or minimal Python-MLX as a stepping stone) IF a quality MLX port of ACE-Step-1.5 exists. Research **DELIVERED 2026-07-16** (docs/research/2026-07-16-ace-mlx-native.md; orchestrator spot-checked the ACEStepClient:16-26 citation — exact, the sidecar speaks ACE-Step's upstream job-queue API): **VERDICT NO-GO on a Swift/mlx-swift helper now — WAIT.** No diffusion-music model class exists in Swift MLX anywhere (mlx-swift-examples = LLM/VLM/embeddings/SD-image only; mlx-audio-swift = TTS/STT/STS/codecs); every existing ACE-Step MLX path (official 1.5 hybrid, `mlx-community/ACE-Step1.5-MLX` weights via mlx-audio, community forks) terminates in Python; cold-start is weight-load-dominated (8–20+ GB from disk — a Swift rewrite pays the same), and the ANE ceiling is confirmed ~1–1.3B params (Core ML SD precedent) vs ACE-Step's 5–9B+ pipeline. RE-CHECK trigger: mlx-audio-swift or mlx-swift-examples shipping ANY diffusion-audio model class. NEAR-TERM alternative flagged and folded into m17-h's decision space (not a separate build): the coarse progress is a limitation of the upstream FastAPI job-queue API — a days-scale Python-only spike driving `mlx_audio`'s loader directly could yield true per-step progress and skip the HTTP queue. This item stays UNCHECKED as the WAIT tripwire; no implementation scheduled.

- [x] (m19-k) **Architecture review: should the live graph follow the device rate at all? (filed from m19-j Task 3).** Today the entire live graph's processing rate follows `outputNode.outputFormat(forBus:0)` — whatever the default device is. A Bluetooth headset entering mic mode drags the whole graph to 24 kHz: every insert chain re-prepares (limiter lookahead halves, PDC recomputes), hosted AUs hit the R7 synchronous renegotiation (bounded main-actor stalls, now bank-gated), and monitoring is band-limited to 12 kHz while the project and every offline render stay 48 kHz. Question: project-rate live graph with explicit mainMixer→outputNode resampling (the pro-DAW norm) — making chain latencies, PDC, and automation math device-invariant by construction (the whole m19-j sensitivity class disappears), with exact live/offline parity and no AU re-prepare storms on device swaps. Known costs (from the m19-j diagnosis): one SRC stage on the live path; a policy question for genuinely high-rate interfaces (96 k); capture edge still needs the device rate; several `graphFormat()` call sites assume graph rate == output-node rate and need a one-by-one audit. Include in scope: the m19-j residual (`Loudness.measure` synchronous on the main actor post-render — hop to a detached task; RenderedAudio is a value type). Route: daw-architect (design first, no code). GATE: a design doc in docs/research/ with a GO/NO-GO and migration plan. — **DONE 2026-07-16, daw-architect single flight, DESIGN-ONLY** (doc is the flight's only write — freeze verified). Doc: `docs/research/2026-07-16-m19k-device-rate-design.md` (572 lines). **Verdict 1 (big question): GO, probe-gated** — both load-bearing SDK behaviors are header-DOCUMENTED, not folklore: AVAudioIONode.h ~:271 "you may set [the input scope] to a different format, in which case the node will convert" and AVAudioEngine.h ~:885-891 "The nodes remain attached and connected with previously set formats … in an output only chain, the output node supports rate conversion" — BOTH quotes verbatim-verified by the orchestrator against the installed SDK. Given the repo's three adverse priors on undocumented AVAudioEngine semantics (m18-d/m16-a/m19-f), the rate flip ships only behind a Phase-0 live probe (5 legs, pass thresholds fixed in-doc so they can't be goal-post-moved, designed fallback shape = documented mixer-input SRC, abort ladder → hybrid alternative with evidence attached). **Verdict 2 (Loudness.measure rider): GO, unconditional** — pure static function over `Sendable` RenderedAudio, all six call sites in `async throws` enclosures (every anchor orch-verified: Render :40/:84/:106/:194/:221, Bounce :117), explicit `Task.detached` hop chosen for SE-0338/SE-0461 executor-semantics immunity, per-site ordering analysis shows zero new hazard classes. Audit: **28 sites/clusters** (16 become project-rate / 5 stay device-coupled — capture edge per documented no-conversion rule + anchor clock-domain math / 7 rate-neutral). **NEW FINDING beyond the filing: today's mid-session flip leaves a rate-incoherence window** — `recoverEngine` (AudioEngine.swift:2126-2160, orch-read) restarts WITHOUT reconnecting, so explicit edges keep the OLD build rate (documented persistence) while `applyParameters` re-prepares every chain at the NEW `graphFormat()` rate (:2150 → PlaybackGraph.swift:1355) — a latent correctness hazard worse than the filed symptom; reasoned-not-reproduced (disclosed), probe leg P4 confirms. Migration plan: Phase 0 probe → Phase 1 inject-the-rate (zero behavior change, incidentally fixes the incoherence window) → Phase 2 flip to 48 k project rate (ITS close-out writes the ARCHITECTURE SETTLED entry — deliberately not written now) → Phase 3 storm-death live gate; rider independent. Implementation seeded as m20-a…e below; honest costs table incl. monitoring-latency probe threshold and the 96 k-interface answer (future `project.sampleRate` field, not device-following).

- [x] (m21-e) **Imported-audio analysis for AI composition (design first).** Today an agent loading a full music file can get transients (`clip.detectTransients`, spectral-flux), loudness (`render.measureLoudness`), master RMS/peak (`mixer.masterAnalysis`), stems (`extract_stems`) and groove (`groove.extract`) — but NO key, chord, tempo, or spectral-balance read, so "add matching tracks to this song" has no harmonic/tempo ground truth (MIDI is fully readable; audio is not). Build `clip.analyzeAudio` (or `audio.analyze`): key estimate (chromagram + Krumhansl-class profile correlation), tempo/beat estimate (onset autocorrelation — reuse the TransientAnalyzer flux front-end), spectral-balance summary (band energies), duration/levels; wire + MCP + copilot catalog so the copilot's first move on imported audio is analyze-then-compose. Offline only, never on the render thread; honest confidence fields (key/tempo estimation is probabilistic — report alternatives, never fake certainty). Design: daw-architect (algorithm choices + accuracy targets against known-key/known-BPM fixtures); implement: audio-dsp-engineer. GATE: fixture suite with known keys/tempos hitting stated accuracy; staging round-trip on a real imported song. — **DESIGN ACCEPTED 2026-07-20** (docs/research/design-clip-analyze-audio.md, 374 lines; orch spot-checked the 3 load-bearing citations — detectClipTransients clipId-only at ProjectStore+Quantize.swift:47, the stretch mapping at :83, spectralFlux `static` reusable in-module): `clip.analyzeAudio {clipId}` clip-based clipId-only (wire 142→143, MCP →146, catalog →60); analyzes the clip's CURRENT SOURCE WINDOW (argued deviation from TransientCache's geometry-free rule — aggregates can't be window-filtered post-hoc; also buys manual per-section analysis); KEY = 16384-pt STFT chroma + Krumhansl-Kessler 24-profile correlation, confidence + `tonal` flag + top-3 (honest ~70% exact / ~90% top-3 expectation stated); TEMPO = spectralFlux reuse verbatim → autocorr → 70-180 lattice fold w/ raw-winner alternate, nullable bpm + `steady` + beat phase; SPECTRAL = same pass, MasterMixAnalyzer bandEdges + 6 macro bands; stretch/pitch echo + derived playback{bpm,key}; throwing engine default (never fabricate); AudioAnalysisCache = TransientCache clone keyed +window@1ms+version; perf ≤2 s typical/5 s hard for 5-min; FIXED accuracy targets in §8. v1 cuts filed: chord tracking, segmentation, live, arbitrary-file, tuning, beat-grid export. Impl: audio-dsp-engineer (engine/DAWCore) then mcp-integration-engineer (wire/MCP/catalog) — queued for the build slot after M22-a. **LEG 1 (engine+store) DONE 2026-07-20, orch-verified 3143/340 0-warn:** AudioContentAnalyzer (one 16384/8192 STFT pass serves chroma+balance, spectralFlux reused verbatim — TransientAnalyzer byte-untouched, diff-confirmed), KeyEstimator (KK 24-rotation, margin-gated confidence, tonal gate), TempoEstimator, AudioAnalysisCache (full discipline suite: single-flight, re-key on window/mtime/version, corrupt self-heal), throwing engine default, ProjectStore.analyzeClipAudio. ALL §8 fixed bars met with honest numbers: 7/7 key fixtures EXACT (conf 0.888-0.937), clicks ±0.05 BPM incl. 44.1 kHz, 60-fold→119.996 w/ 60+240 alternates, phase 9.6 ms (bar 25), tempo-change steady:false, pink bpm:nil + slope −3.017, centroid 1000.00, silence exact −80s, levels ±0.001 dB, window-contract both halves; perf **1.19 s for 5-min** (bar ≤2 typical).
